# design

## accessibility
CL 1
L design v1 -- Focus, labels, keyboard and contrast
T t1 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t2{content:str sha256:str ..}
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t1) -> t2 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
C workspace.read focus-css:matches(workspace.read(path:cssPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"outline:\\s*(none|0)|:focus(-visible)?\\s*\\{[^}]*box-shadow:\\s*none"}}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read a11y-jsx:matches(workspace.read(path:jsxPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"<(div|span|li)\\b[^>]*\\bonClick="}}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read focus-css:matches(workspace.read(path:cssPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"outline:\\s*(none|0)|:focus(-visible)?\\s*\\{[^}]*box-shadow:\\s*none"}}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read a11y-jsx:matches(workspace.read(path:jsxPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"<(div|span|li)\\b[^>]*\\bonClick="}}] type:"string"})
P check-access(cssPath:str jsxPath:str):css=workspace.read(path:cssPath) C focus-css; jsx=workspace.read(path:jsxPath) C a11y-jsx; J keyboard-path -- Verify focus is kept in CSS and controls are real elements in JSX, then judge keyboard reachability
V P check-access -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
J keyboard-path yes|fix:"Can every action be reached and used with the keyboard alone, with a visible focus ring?" -- Tab through the rendered component in a browser tab. preview.taste lists unnamed controls and small targets; any unnamed control is fix.
V J keyboard-path -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X outline: none without a replacement -> Delete it; the .nx focus ring already applies
X Tooltip text used as the only label -> Pass label to IconButton (aria-label + title)
F Focus-not-obscured (2.4.11) and drag alternatives (2.5.7) have no automatic check yet: they need a Tab walk and an Arrange journey in the taste lens.
M design "Focus is never removed: .nx :focus-visible draws var(--nx-focus) (2px background gap + accent ring). Custom controls keep it; inputs may use inset 0 0 0 1px var(--nx-accent-hi) on :focus." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Every icon-only control has a label (IconButton label=...); decorative icons are aria-hidden (Icon does it)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Real elements: <button type=button> for actions, <a> for navigation, <label> around inputs. Choice groups use role=radiogroup + aria-checked (Segmented); menus/lists use role=menuitem/option with useRovingKeys." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Dialogs: role=dialog + aria-label, Escape closes, focus moves inside and returns to the opener (Popover and Sheet do this)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Colour is never the only signal: a dot has a word, an error has an icon and text." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Keyboard: Tab reaches every action in reading order; Enter/Space activate; nothing needs hover only." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Contrast: body text uses --nx-text or --nx-text-2, small labels and counts --nx-muted (4.5:1 or better); --nx-faint fails for small text. Morning is where light tints wash out; check it." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Live regions: toasts already announce; a status that changes in place uses aria-live=polite." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "WCAG 2.2 (after Addy Osmani's accessibility skill): targets at least 24x24 px (2.5.8), 36 px icon buttons on phone; focus is never hidden under a sticky header, the dock or a toast (2.4.11), so scroll areas get scroll-padding; every drag (Arrange mode, reordering) has a non-drag way to do the same, buttons or keys (2.5.7)." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## check-loop
CL 1
L design v1 -- Render, look, fix: dev UI, light and dark, phone, console, build, proof
T t1 [json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"desktop\",\"phone\"]}"]
T t2 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"dark\",\"light\"]}"
T t3 json:"{\"type\":\"boolean\",\"description\":\"Inline capped screenshots for the installed desktop Preview, which has no HTTP artifact route.\"}"
T t4 json:"{\"type\":\"object\",\"description\":\"Controls to exercise through Laya: actions [{kind: click|fill|select|press, label, value?, expect?}], expect [{kind: selector_text|text_present|selector_exists|url_contains, ...}].\"}"
T t5 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"description\":\"Optional report directory inside the active workspace. Omit to use the managed artifact directory.\"}"
T t6{gate:str score?:int report:str screenshots:json:"{\"type\":\"object\",\"properties\":{\"desktop\":{\"type\":\"object\",\"properties\":{\"path\":{\"type\":\"string\"}},\"required\":[\"path\"]}}}" ..}
T t7 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t8{content:str sha256:str ..}
T t9 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"domcontentloaded\",\"load\",\"networkidle\"]}"
T t10{path:str width:int height:int ..}
T t11 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"diff\",\"file\",\"artifact\",\"terminal\",\"browser\",\"mission\",\"replay\",\"builder\",\"accounts\",\"runtime\",\"settings\",\"preview\",\"outputs\",\"perception\"]}"
T t12{kind:str target:str ..}
T t13 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"info\",\"success\",\"warning\",\"error\"]}"
T t14{message:str ..}
T t15 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"auto\",\"powershell\",\"python\",\"bash\",\"cmd\"]}"
T t16{exitCode:0 stdout?:str ..}
A preview.taste(url:str goal?:str#..600 viewports?:t1 colorScheme?:t2 waitFor?:str delayMs?:0..10000 includeImageData?:t3 journey?:t4 outputDir?:t5) -> t6 ! -- Render desktop 1440x900 and phone 390x844 in the given colour scheme; write screenshots + taste-report.json (gate, findings, contrast, overflow, unnamed controls)
C preview.taste light-rendered:matches(workspace.read(path:result.report) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"\"gate\": \"blocked\""}} {not:{pattern:"\"measured\": 0,"}}] type:"string"})
C preview.taste dark-rendered:matches(workspace.read(path:result.report) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"\"gate\": \"blocked\""}} {not:{pattern:"\"measured\": 0,"}}] type:"string"})
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t7) -> t8 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
C workspace.read component-css:matches(workspace.read(path:cssPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\\b|\\b(rgba?|hsla?)\\("}} {not:{pattern:"transition:\\s*all|\\b[0-9]*\\.?[0-9]+m?s\\b"}} {not:{pattern:"(^|[^-])width:\\s*([4-9][0-9]{2}|[0-9]{4,})px|min-width:\\s*([4-9][0-9]{2}|[0-9]{4,})px|overflow-x:\\s*scroll"}} {not:{pattern:"outline:\\s*(none|0)|:focus(-visible)?\\s*\\{[^}]*box-shadow:\\s*none"}} {pattern:"var\\(--nx-"}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read component-jsx:matches(workspace.read(path:jsxPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\\b|\\b(rgba?|hsla?)\\("}} {not:{pattern:"style=\\{\\{[^}]*(color|background|border|boxShadow)"}} {not:{pattern:"<(div|span|li)\\b[^>]*\\bonClick="}} {not:{pattern:"[a-z]!['\\\"<]"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)click here"}} {pattern:"\\bnx-"}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read build-passed:workspace.read(path:".agent_control/build-check.log") .content has "built in"
C workspace.read build-entrypoint:workspace.read(path:".agent_control/build-check/index.html") .content has "<html"
A preview.screenshot(url:str outputPath?:str fullPage?:bool width?:320..3840 height?:240..2160 waitFor?:str delayMs?:0..10000 waitUntil?:t9) -> t10 ! -- Write one PNG and return its path, size and SHA-256
F verify-screenshot "No authored observer check is bound to preview.screenshot" -> ask operator blocks:preview.screenshot
A neyvia.pane.show(kind:t11 target:str) -> t12 ! -- Open that pane on Paul's stage beside the chat
F verify-pane-show "No authored observer check is bound to neyvia.pane.show" -> ask operator blocks:neyvia.pane.show
A neyvia.notify(message?:str msg?:str level?:t13) -> t14 -- Show a toast in Neyvia
F verify-notify "No authored observer check is bound to neyvia.notify" -> ask operator blocks:neyvia.notify
A terminal.exec(command:str#1.. shell?:t15 cwd?:str timeoutMs?:1..120000 maxOutputChars?:128..50000) -> t16 ! -- Run installed Vite synchronously with a runner config and cache/output confined to the owned test root; return only after the actual build completes.
F verify-build "No authored observer check is bound to terminal.exec" -> ask operator blocks:terminal.exec
C workspace.read light-rendered:matches(workspace.read(path:light.report) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"\"gate\": \"blocked\""}} {not:{pattern:"\"measured\": 0,"}}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read dark-rendered:matches(workspace.read(path:dark.report) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"\"gate\": \"blocked\""}} {not:{pattern:"\"measured\": 0,"}}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read component-css:matches(workspace.read(path:cssPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\\b|\\b(rgba?|hsla?)\\("}} {not:{pattern:"transition:\\s*all|\\b[0-9]*\\.?[0-9]+m?s\\b"}} {not:{pattern:"(^|[^-])width:\\s*([4-9][0-9]{2}|[0-9]{4,})px|min-width:\\s*([4-9][0-9]{2}|[0-9]{4,})px|overflow-x:\\s*scroll"}} {not:{pattern:"outline:\\s*(none|0)|:focus(-visible)?\\s*\\{[^}]*box-shadow:\\s*none"}} {pattern:"var\\(--nx-"}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read component-jsx:matches(workspace.read(path:jsxPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\\b|\\b(rgba?|hsla?)\\("}} {not:{pattern:"style=\\{\\{[^}]*(color|background|border|boxShadow)"}} {not:{pattern:"<(div|span|li)\\b[^>]*\\bonClick="}} {not:{pattern:"[a-z]!['\\\"<]"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)click here"}} {pattern:"\\bnx-"}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read build-passed:workspace.read(path:".agent_control/build-check.log") .content has "built in"
C workspace.read build-entrypoint:workspace.read(path:".agent_control/build-check/index.html") .content has "<html"
P check-render(url:str waitFor:str):light=preview.taste(colorScheme:"light" url:url viewports:["desktop" "phone"] waitFor:waitFor) C light-rendered; dark=preview.taste(colorScheme:"dark" url:url viewports:["desktop" "phone"] waitFor:waitFor) C dark-rendered; J visual=ship; shown=neyvia.pane.show(kind:"artifact" target:light.screenshots.desktop.path) -- Render a page in light and dark at desktop and phone width, refuse blocked or blank renders, look, and show the proof
V P check-render -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
P check-component(cssPath:str jsxPath:str url:str waitFor:str):css=workspace.read(path:cssPath) C component-css; jsx=workspace.read(path:jsxPath) C component-jsx; light=preview.taste(colorScheme:"light" url:url viewports:["desktop" "phone"] waitFor:waitFor) C light-rendered; dark=preview.taste(colorScheme:"dark" url:url viewports:["desktop" "phone"] waitFor:waitFor) C dark-rendered; J visual -- Verify a component's CSS and JSX against the token, motion, layout, focus and copy rules, then render its design-lab specimen in light and dark
V P check-component -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
P build-check():started=terminal.exec(command:"import runpy; runpy.run_path('scripts/c8e_design_checks.py')['run']('build', '.')" shell:"python" timeoutMs:120000); J build-wait=finished; log=workspace.read(path:".agent_control/build-check.log") C build-passed; entrypoint=workspace.read(path:".agent_control/build-check/index.html") C build-entrypoint -- Build the actual configured candidate source into a fresh owned output, wait for completion, and read the build log and emitted entrypoint.
V P build-check -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
J visual ship|revise:"Looking at the screenshots, is it calm, clear and right in both themes and at phone width?" -- Open every PNG. Name where the eye lands first and what competes; check Morning contrast, phone wraps, doubled borders. Any block finding or overflow is revise. A clear gate with nothing visible is a blank render, not a pass.
V J visual -> model:large evidence:screenshot why:"model judgment bound to current evidence"
J build-wait finished|failed:"Has the build finished?" -- The scoped builder waits for actual exit. finished requires retained build log plus real emitted entrypoint; failed requires inspecting the cause before retrying.
V J build-wait -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X vite build run through terminal.exec directly times out (120 s cap; measured on 2 Oct) -> Use build-check: it starts the build detached and checks the log
X Relative --outDir fails at the end (the desktop-loader plugin resolves it from the working folder) -> Pass an absolute path ($PWD in PowerShell), as build-check does
X Geist fonts answer 403 in a worktree whose node_modules is a junction, so screenshots show Segoe UI -> vite.config.mjs allows the junction's real path; if fonts still fail, check the network tab before judging type
X preview.taste returns gate 'clear', score 100 on a blank page (nothing rendered yet) -> Pass waitFor with a selector that only exists after render; the *-rendered checks refuse reports where nothing was measured
X colorScheme light but the full shell still shows Forest -> The shell uses its own saved theme (Forest in a fresh browser); test components in the design lab, which follows colorScheme or ?theme
X Building into web/dist on a track branch -> Use build-check (it builds into .agent_control/build-check); web/dist is release output
X Console errors ignored because the screenshot looks fine -> Read console errors in a browser tab before claiming done
F preview.taste cannot press Tab; keyboard and focus checks need a real browser tab
F build-check runs in the selected workspace's root; outside a Neyvia checkout it fails, which is the right answer
F White on --nx-accent in Forest measures 3.5:1 (preview.taste warns on primary buttons); changing the accent is Paul's call
M design "Loop: build -> render -> look at the PNGs -> fix -> render again. A passing script or a 'clear' gate is not a look." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Dev UI: npx vite --config vite.config.mjs --port <your port> with FLUXIO_WEB_BACKEND_URL=http://127.0.0.1:<your backend>. Use only the ports assigned to you." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Components alone: /design-lab.html renders every file in web/src/neyvia/next/lab/specimens/*.jsx (default export + `export const title`) inside a real .nx root. ?theme=dark|light|sunset|night, ?only=<file name>; without ?theme it follows the browser: light = Morning, dark = Forest." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Render with preview.taste(url, viewports [desktop, phone], colorScheme light|dark, waitFor a selector that exists only after content renders, e.g. [data-specimen]) -> gate, findings, screenshots, taste-report.json." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Light and dark: in the lab use colorScheme (or ?theme). The full shell keeps its own saved theme (Forest in a fresh browser), so colorScheme alone does not switch it." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Phone: the phone viewport is 390x844; horizontalOverflowPx must be 0, no text collisions, no unnamed controls." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Console clean: open the page in your own browser tab and read console errors and React warnings; fix them before proof." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Build: build-check starts npx vite build detached (it takes 40-90 s; terminal.exec stops at 120 s), writes .agent_control/build-check.log and builds into .agent_control/build-check (absolute $PWD path, git-ignored). Wait for 'built in' or an error, then resume. Never build into web/dist from a track branch." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Proof: one screenshot per claim (light + dark; phone when layout changed), shown to Paul with pane.show kind artifact, plus a one-line notify." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Look for: where the eye lands first and whether that's right, what competes with it, bad wraps, doubled borders, washed-out Morning text, the focus ring on Tab." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## copy
CL 1
L design v1 -- UI words: plain, short, friendly, in Paul's words
T t1 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t2{content:str sha256:str ..}
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t1) -> t2 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
C workspace.read copy-jsx:matches(workspace.read(path:jsxPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"[a-z]!['\\\"<]"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)click here"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)\\berror:\\s"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)something went wrong"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)\\b(oops|uh oh|sorry)\\b"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)\\b(seamless(ly)?|unleash|elevate|revolutioni[sz]e|supercharge|effortless(ly)?)\\b"}} {not:{pattern:"[\\\"'][A-Za-z][^\\\"']* [\\\"']\\s*\\+\\s*\\w+\\s*\\+\\s*[\\\"']"}}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read copy-jsx:matches(workspace.read(path:jsxPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"[a-z]!['\\\"<]"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)click here"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)\\berror:\\s"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)something went wrong"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)\\b(oops|uh oh|sorry)\\b"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)\\b(seamless(ly)?|unleash|elevate|revolutioni[sz]e|supercharge|effortless(ly)?)\\b"}} {not:{pattern:"[\\\"'][A-Za-z][^\\\"']* [\\\"']\\s*\\+\\s*\\w+\\s*\\+\\s*[\\\"']"}}] type:"string"})
P review-copy(jsxPath:str):jsx=workspace.read(path:jsxPath) C copy-jsx; J copy-plain -- Check a component's visible words against the copy rules and decide keep or rewrite
V P review-copy -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
J copy-plain keep|rewrite:"Would Paul say these words out loud, and are they as short as they can be?" -- Read every string in the JSX. Remove words before shrinking type. One idea per sentence. Same term for the same thing across the shell.
V J copy-plain -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X Developer words leak into the UI (payload, session id, null) -> Translate to what Paul sees; keep ids behind a Copy button
X Generic error text -> Name what failed and the next step: 'Couldn't reach your PC. Check it's on, then try again.'
M design "Plain, short, friendly, like Paul talking. Examples from the app: 'New chat', 'Open', 'Use plan limits', 'That code didn't work. Start the sign-in again.'" src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Sentence case everywhere; capitals only for names. Uppercase only through .nx-tag." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Buttons are verbs (Open, Sign in, Archive, Try again); titles are nouns; at most one sentence of help under a title." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Errors say what happened and what to do next. No codes, stack traces or 'Error:' prefixes on screen; keep details in logs or a Copy button." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "No jargon where Paul reads it: chat (not session/thread), PC (not host), changes (not diff), add-on pack (not capability bundle), sign in (not authenticate)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Times read 'now', '3m', 'Working 3m' (ago/elapsed); counts are digits; sizes like '2.4 MB'." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "No exclamation marks, emoji, 'Click here', marketing words or apologies. Say what is true, including 'Not set up yet' and 'Coming'." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Reuse the word already used for the same thing elsewhere in the shell (launcher, sidebar, settings)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "A confirm repeats the consequence ('Delete project?' offers 'Delete project' and 'Cancel'); a toggle names its ON state ('Send read receipts', never a negative); an action keeps its name through a flow ('Publish' then 'Published') (after Jakub Krehel's better-writing and Anthropic's frontend-design)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Never glue a sentence around a variable ('You have ' + n + ' messages'); write one template with its plural forms. Placeholders show an example ('name@example.com'), never the only label." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Status and progress lead with the next action and say where things are ('Step 3 of 5 done: schema updated. Next: backfill.'); show at most five items before 'Show all'; give times in units ('About 15 min'), never 'a bit' (after Ayoub Ghriss's i-have-adhd; the same style fits Neyvia's replies to Paul)." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## craft
CL 1
L design v1 -- Craft check: run the design-craft CL-Skill on a component and record the judgements
T t1 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t2 json:"{\"type\":\"object\"}"
T t3 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"auto\",\"powershell\",\"python\",\"bash\",\"cmd\"]}"
T t4{exitCode:int stdout?:str ..}
T t5{content:str sha256:str ..}
A workspace.write(path:str#1.. content:str expectedSha256?:t1) -> t2 ! -- Save the check request: JSON {files: [jsx, css], url: design-lab URL with ?only=<specimen>, journal: path of the J lines}
F verify-request "No authored observer check is bound to workspace.write" -> ask operator blocks:workspace.write
A terminal.exec(command:str#1.. shell?:t3 cwd?:str timeoutMs?:1..120000 maxOutputChars?:128..50000) -> t4 ! -- Run the production CL-Skill checks through the pinned C8e adapter on actual files and rendered specimens, using the existing headless Playwright capture runtime at the explicit candidate origin.
F verify-run "No authored observer check is bound to terminal.exec" -> ask operator blocks:terminal.exec
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t1) -> t5 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
F verify-read "No authored observer check is bound to workspace.read" -> ask operator blocks:workspace.read
J craft-judged recorded|missing:"Have you answered every J line of design-craft (motion-need, emphasis, states, removal, copy-voice) in the journal?" -- One line each in the journal file: J <name>=<option> "<one-line reason>". An unanswered J lowers procedure adherence and shows as a Q line in the receipt.
V J craft-judged -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X Every rendered check is ?undecided (render failed or nothing measured) -> Fix a -compiles failure first; then make sure the dev UI is up and no other specimen breaks the design lab; pass the design-lab URL with ?only=<specimen>
X The receipt says fail although the screen looks fine -> Read each X line: it names the file, line and the fix. Block checks encode Neyvia's direction and do not bend; a warn that is wrong for this case goes to manual.frontier with the evidence
F Outcome quality is still a person or a large model looking at the screenshots; the Evolver's fitness (adherence x quality / tokens) needs that score per run.
M design "design-craft is the first CL-Skill (docs/standard/cl-skill.md): Neyvia's design rules plus the reviewed seed skills (Anthropic frontend-design, Emil Kowalski's motion skills, Meng To's audit and shadows, Addy Osmani's accessibility, Jakub Krehel's better-writing, i-have-adhd, antislop, taste-skill, baseline-ui), compiled to 50 checks, 5 judgement points and 2 procedures. Sources, commits and licences are its M lines." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Run it from a shell too: python -m grant_agent.cl_skill check --files <jsx> <css> --url http://localhost:<vite>/design-lab.html?only=<specimen> --journal run.cl (PYTHONPATH=src). describe --level 0|1|2 prints the skill at a level." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Receipt: R design-craft.build ok|fail|unknown adherence:<0-1> output:<checks> procedure:<judgements> +pass -fail ?undecided; one X line per failure with file:line and the fix; one Q line per undecided check or missing judgement." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Adherence = output x (0.8 + 0.2 x procedure); output weighs block 3, warn 1, note 0.25 over the checks that decided. ok means no block failed and every check decided." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "The vendored skill texts (docs/skills/vendor) are reference data: never run the install, update or telemetry commands inside them." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## details
CL 1
L design v1 -- Details library: rolling numbers, meters, confirm in place, strike, copy, empty and calm loading states, press and haptics
T t1 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t2{content:str sha256:str ..}
T t3 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"auto\",\"powershell\",\"python\",\"bash\",\"cmd\"]}"
T t4{exitCode:int stdout?:str ..}
T t5 json:"{\"type\":\"object\"}"
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t1) -> t2 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
F verify-read "No authored observer check is bound to workspace.read" -> ask operator blocks:workspace.read
A terminal.exec(command:str#1.. shell?:t3 cwd?:str timeoutMs?:1..120000 maxOutputChars?:128..50000) -> t4 ! -- Run existing production lab/kit probes using the headless Playwright renderer; shell proof creates, keeps and deletes an actual saved scene and checks persistence. No mock bus or synthetic frontend model.
F verify-run "No authored observer check is bound to terminal.exec" -> ask operator blocks:terminal.exec
A workspace.write(path:str#1.. content:str expectedSha256?:t1) -> t5 ! -- Save the proof request: JSON {url: dev UI origin, out?: folder}
F verify-request "No authored observer check is bound to workspace.write" -> ask operator blocks:workspace.write
C workspace.read details-credited:matches(workspace.read(path:"docs/design/details-library.md") .content {allOf:[{pattern:"https://rareui\\.com"} {pattern:"MIT \\+ Commons Clause"} {pattern:"barvian/number-flow"} {pattern:"ibelick/motion-primitives"}] type:"string"})
J detail-fit none|rolling-number|meter|confirm-in-place|strike|copy|empty-state|calm-loading:"Which detail does this moment need?" -- none is the default (Emil's frequency gate: 100+/day or keyboard moments get no motion). rolling-number = a count that changes while you look (done steps, tasks); not for prices or IDs. meter = a fraction you can trust: real value/max, or an estimate that stays under 90 % until done; never a fake percentage. confirm-in-place = an action that cannot be undone and has no Undo toast; recoverable actions (Recycle Bin, archive with Undo) do not ask. strike = a finished item that stays visible. copy = any copy control (it must say Copied or Copy failed). empty-state = nothing to show: say what is missing and one way forward. calm-loading = any loading state that can finish in under a quarter second.
V J detail-fit -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X A rolling number's faces show up in the page text (an agent or a test reads '0123456789…') -> Faces are CSS-generated (::before content); keep the visually hidden full number as the only DOM text
X A column jumps instead of rolling, or rolls nine faces the wrong way -> Never re-insert a rolling column into the page (it loses its transition); move only nodes whose place changed; direction comes from the value's change (wheelTarget)
X A progress bar reaches 100 % while the work still runs -> Use Meter with startedAt/estimateMs (creep stays under 90 %) and set done only from the real result
X A confirm dialog for an action that has Undo -> Do it and offer Undo (the Tidy toast pattern); ConfirmInPlace is only for the irreversible
X The skeleton flashes for 100 ms on every fast load -> Gate it with useCalmLoading (240 ms delay, 480 ms minimum)
X A silent copy button -> CopyButton or useCopy: drawn check plus 'Copied', and 'Copy failed' when the clipboard refuses
F design-craft's disclosure-state check flags ConfirmInPlace (setOpen without aria-expanded): the trigger is replaced by the question, not expanded, so aria-expanded would always read false. Narrowing candidate (CL-Skill section 7), left as a warn on record.
F The kit is web/PWA only. Expo/React Native and the desktop template need their own versions (Reanimated for the wheel, Haptics.selectionAsync for haptic).
F NxFilesApp's copy path uses CopyButton but was not driven live (the Files app needs a signed-in backend; dev fixtures do not cover it); the same component is driven in the chat and the lab.
F Rare UI's licence (MIT + Commons Clause + attribution) forbids redistributing its components; Neyvia's details are original code that takes ideas only, and credit Rare UI visibly anyway. If Paul reads the clause as covering ideas, set template:false on the four rare-ui entries in details.manifest.json.
M design "Import from ./details/nxDetails.jsx (one CSS file comes with it). Generated web apps get the same details from details/details.js through the App SDK template (details.manifest.json 'kit')." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "RollingNumber({value, decimals?, locale?, prefix?, suffix?}): one wheel per digit, rolls the short way on --nx-settle with the settle spring; a new place fades in; screen readers hear the whole number once. Strings only for prefix/suffix." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Meter({value, max} | {startedAt, estimateMs}, done?, tone: ok|running|needs|error, label, decorative?): transform-only fill on --nx-drift; decorative when the words beside it already say the same." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "ConfirmInPlace({question, confirmLabel, keepLabel='Keep', doneText, onConfirm, children: ask => trigger}): the question replaces the trigger; focus on Keep; Escape or a click elsewhere keeps and hands focus back to the same control; Delete announces doneText." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Strike({on}): a 1px line drawn through each line of the text on --nx-settle; the text turns muted. Wrap the words in an inline element, not a flex item." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "CopyButton({text, label, showLabel?, size: sm|xs}) and useCopy(): Copy icon to a check revealed left to right, 'Copied' or 'Copy failed' announced, back after 1.6 s." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "EmptyState({icon, title, hint, action?: {label, onClick, icon?}, tone: first|filtered|done}): one mark, one line, one hint, at most one action; the action does something real." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "useCalmLoading(loading, {delay=240, min=480}) -> boolean: render the loading state only when it returns true." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "PRESS ('nx-press' class) on Button/IconButton/CopyButton: scale(0.97) on press with the snappy spring. haptic('tick'|'confirm'): 8 ms vibration on touch phones only, never with reduced motion. Keyboard focus is never animated." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Sources: Rare UI (Swami Malode, MIT + Commons Clause + attribution, rareui.com: animated-counter, delete-button, task-list, grid-reveal ideas), NumberFlow (Maxwell Barvian, MIT: mask and digit technique), motion-primitives (ibelick, MIT: studied, text-shimmer rejected as a new loop), Sonner and Emil Kowalski's skills (MIT: press scale, exit faster than enter). All Neyvia code is original; docs/design/details-library.md has the full notes." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## direction
CL 1
L design v1 -- Visual direction: cool deep backgrounds, rare warm accents, the tree and sun
T t1{results:json:"{\"type\":\"array\"}" ..}
A ui.inspiration.search(query:str surface?:str platform?:str style?:str color?:str layout?:str limit?:1..24) -> t1 -- Return an image board of references with facets; nothing is copied
F verify-inspiration "No authored observer check is bound to ui.inspiration.search" -> ask operator blocks:ui.inspiration.search
P find-references(query:str):board=ui.inspiration.search(limit:12 query:query); J reference-transfer -- Look at strong references for a surface and keep only principles that fit Neyvia
V P find-references -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
J reference-transfer adopt-principle|reject:"Does a reference carry a principle that solves a real problem here?" -- Prefer shipped tools used daily over concept shots. Write the principle and where it would fail; translate it into Neyvia tokens and primitives. Never copy a surface, palette or logo.
V J reference-transfer -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X Brown or muddy darks -> Cool the hue toward green/teal/violet and deepen it
X Orange everywhere to feel warm -> Warmth is the sun: one hero action and states only
X Dribbble-style glass, giant type, fake charts -> Real product state, compact type, honest empty states
X Every block gets an eyebrow label, a numbered marker or an icon tile -> Run the removal test; keep it only if it carries information
F Paul's verdict on a new look only comes from him seeing it; show screenshots and ask before spreading a new pattern
M design "Cool, deep backgrounds: Forest near-black green, Sunset twilight violet, Night Green dark water teal; Morning is warm paper with deep leaf greens. Warm colour is rare and means something." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "The sun means something is happening (amber running, gold needs you, the sun gradient for the one hero action). The calm working surface and its actions are green." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Identity: the warm sun-and-tree mark (scripts/brand/neyvia_sun_mark.py) and the growing tree for work. The logo is warm; the UI stays green. Don't recolour the UI to match the logo." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Paul's taste so far: likes Morning; rejected the first muddy dark theme and a brown/maroon Sunset. No brown or maroon surfaces; keep darks cool and deep." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Calm first: few boxes, tonal fills over borders, one accent per area, detail on demand. If removing 20-40% of borders, pills and helper lines loses nothing, remove them." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Hierarchy must survive greyscale: size, weight and spacing carry it; colour carries meaning only." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Newsreader display type only for a title that greets (Accounts 'You', the new-chat title); the rest is Geist." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Blur/glass only where something real shows through (Sunset and Night Green widgets); never decorative." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Familiar grammar for typing, menus, files, back/close; spend novelty where Neyvia differs (agents at work, live checklists, movable dashboard)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Layout feel: like Xiaomi HyperOS, every surface is movable and the rest reflows; design components that work in more than one place." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Spend boldness in one place: one element per screen carries the emphasis (the sun action or a status); everything around it stays quiet. Structure is information: numbers only for real sequences, a label only when the content does not already say it (after Anthropic's frontend-design)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Two generated-default looks sit close to Neyvia on purpose: warm paper with a serif display (Morning with Newsreader) and near-black with a green accent (Forest). Keep them chosen, not default: Newsreader only for greeting titles, green deep and calm (never acid), no terracotta or brass accents, no tracked uppercase eyebrows." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Removal test before shipping (after Meng To's audit-ai-design-slop): for each border, pill, label, icon and helper line, say what information, state or action it carries; if removing it loses nothing, remove it." src:"authored manual" state:verified

CL 1
L design v1 -- Fast mounted surface outcomes
T t1 json:"{\"type\":\"object\"}"
A neyvia.view.state() -> t1 -- Read current rendered surface observations
C neyvia.view.state fresh:neyvia.view.state() .state.fresh == true
C neyvia.view.state panels-opaque:neyvia.view.state() .state.dom.outcomes.panelsOpaque == true
C neyvia.view.state chips-unclipped:neyvia.view.state() .state.dom.outcomes.chipsUnclipped == true
C neyvia.view.state visible-copy-clean:neyvia.view.state() .state.dom.outcomes.copyClean == true
C neyvia.view.state panels-opaque:neyvia.view.state() .state.dom.outcomes.panelsOpaque == true
C neyvia.view.state chips-unclipped:neyvia.view.state() .state.dom.outcomes.chipsUnclipped == true
C neyvia.view.state visible-copy-clean:neyvia.view.state() .state.dom.outcomes.copyClean == true
C neyvia.view.state fresh:neyvia.view.state() .state.fresh == true

V P verify-panels-opaque -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
P verify-chips-unclipped():fresh=neyvia.view.state() C fresh; observed=neyvia.view.state() C chips-unclipped -- chips-unclipped

## layout
CL 1
L design v1 -- Gutters, phone width, no sideways scroll, movable surfaces
T t1 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t2{content:str sha256:str ..}
T t3 [json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"desktop\",\"phone\"]}"]
T t4 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"dark\",\"light\"]}"
T t5 json:"{\"type\":\"boolean\",\"description\":\"Inline capped screenshots for the installed desktop Preview, which has no HTTP artifact route.\"}"
T t6 json:"{\"type\":\"object\",\"description\":\"Controls to exercise through Laya: actions [{kind: click|fill|select|press, label, value?, expect?}], expect [{kind: selector_text|text_present|selector_exists|url_contains, ...}].\"}"
T t7 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"description\":\"Optional report directory inside the active workspace. Omit to use the managed artifact directory.\"}"
T t8{gate:str score?:int report:str screenshots:json:"{\"type\":\"object\",\"properties\":{\"desktop\":{\"type\":\"object\",\"properties\":{\"path\":{\"type\":\"string\"}},\"required\":[\"path\"]}}}" ..}
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t1) -> t2 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
C workspace.read layout-css:matches(workspace.read(path:cssPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"(^|[^-])width:\\s*([4-9][0-9]{2}|[0-9]{4,})px|min-width:\\s*([4-9][0-9]{2}|[0-9]{4,})px|overflow-x:\\s*scroll"}}] type:"string"})
A preview.taste(url:str goal?:str#..600 viewports?:t3 colorScheme?:t4 waitFor?:str delayMs?:0..10000 includeImageData?:t5 journey?:t6 outputDir?:t7) -> t8 ! -- Render desktop 1440x900 and phone 390x844 in the given colour scheme; write screenshots + taste-report.json (gate, findings, contrast, overflow, unnamed controls)
C preview.taste phone-rendered:matches(workspace.read(path:result.report) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"\"gate\": \"blocked\""}} {not:{pattern:"\"measured\": 0,"}}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read layout-css:matches(workspace.read(path:cssPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"(^|[^-])width:\\s*([4-9][0-9]{2}|[0-9]{4,})px|min-width:\\s*([4-9][0-9]{2}|[0-9]{4,})px|overflow-x:\\s*scroll"}}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read phone-rendered:matches(workspace.read(path:phone.report) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"\"gate\": \"blocked\""}} {not:{pattern:"\"measured\": 0,"}}] type:"string"})
P phone-check(cssPath:str url:str waitFor:str):css=workspace.read(path:cssPath) C layout-css; phone=preview.taste(colorScheme:"light" url:url viewports:["phone"] waitFor:waitFor) C phone-rendered; J phone-fit -- Verify a screen's CSS has no wide fixed widths, render it at phone width and decide if it fits
V P phone-check -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
J phone-fit fits|rework:"Does the phone screenshot read well at 390px?" -- Open the PNG and look: no sideways scroll, no clipped or colliding text, primary action reachable without hunting, nothing under 11px. horizontalOverflowPx > 0 in the report is always rework.
V J phone-fit -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
J placement-look fits|rework:"Does every app read well in every placement?" -- Open docs/evidence/placement-shots/index.html and look at every row: each window fills its place with its title bar and placement buttons, nothing clipped or colliding, narrow places use the app's compact layout, the shell under a full-screen window is hidden.
V J placement-look -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
J app-identity fits|rework:"Does every app look like itself, and beautiful, in every theme?" -- Open docs/evidence/appdesign/after/sheet-a.png and sheet-b.png (and the final/ pairs). Each app must read as itself in all four themes: terminal ink, PDF paper on a desk, warm notes, graphite 3D, neutral image studio, App Factory showing the app in a frame. Nothing inside an app is painted the theme's green; no text vanishes in Morning; side panel and bubble use the compact layouts. Obscura paints no iframes, WebGL or web fonts: those are judged in a real browser.
V J app-identity -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X A grid track or chip forces the page wider than the phone -> minmax(0, 1fr), min-width: 0 and flex-wrap on the offender
X Hover-only tools unreachable on touch -> Keep them visible under @media (hover: none)
M design "Phone = max-width 760px: the shell sets .is-phone on the root (useMedia in NxShell.jsx). Style it with .nx.is-phone .nx-<part>, not a second component." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Side gutters 16px: content padding 0 16px; panel/tool rows 48px tall with padding 0 8px 0 16px. Readable columns width: min(720px, 100%) centred." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "No sideways scroll at 390px: grid tracks minmax(0, 1fr), flex children min-width: 0, long names text-overflow: ellipsis or overflow-wrap: anywhere, chip rows flex-wrap: wrap." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Two columns become one under @media (max-width: 640px). Fixed widths above 400px are never used; use max-width or min()." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Phone touch: icon buttons 36px (.nx.is-phone .nx-iconbtn), inputs font-size 16px so iOS doesn't zoom, padding respects --nx-safe-top / --nx-safe-bottom." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Region widths are tokens: --nx-sidebar-w, --nx-panel-w, --nx-dock-w, --nx-thread-w, --nx-canopy-w." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Spacing steps 2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 20, 24px: 6-8 inside a row, 10-14 between cards, 12-16 card padding." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Surfaces are movable like a dashboard (Arrange mode, nxLayoutModel.js; neyvia.view.arrange): home cards use .nx-widget and reflow; never assume a fixed position or neighbour." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Scroll areas: one scroller per region (.nx-scroll, min-height: 0 on the flex parent); headers and composers stay put (flex: none)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Apps and panes are windows (nxPlacementModel.js, NxPlacement.jsx): open them with os.openApp / os.showPane and never render a stage app inside a region yourself. One layer draws every window over its slot, so a move never remounts the app or reloads its iframes." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Every window offers the same four places with the same controls: title-bar buttons, title-bar drag to drop zones, Alt+Shift+arrows (Home = beside the chat), Esc out of full screen, bubble keys Enter/R/Delete/arrows." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "A narrow window (side panel, bubble peek, under 640px) is not a phone: read useSurfaceSize().compact (nxSurfaceSize.js) or .nx-surface[data-compact] for a compact layout, not only the viewport media query." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "App Factory, App preview and CiteCraft read their own window, not the screen: App Factory switches to one column (the app first, Brief and Build behind tabs) under 760px of window; native studios stack through @container nxstudio; the App preview canvas shrinks its frame. useSurfaceSize().compact is there for apps that need it in JavaScript." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "An app that makes things shows the thing (Paul: 'the App Factory doesn't show the thing being made'): App Factory and App preview draw the app in a frame that fits its target (TargetFrame: a window for native desktop, a browser for a Neyvia local app, an iPhone or Android phone), with a badge saying what it is (Sample, Sketch of your brief, Building, Live) and the build rail flowing under it." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## learned-capabilities
CL 1
L design v1 -- Calibrated LAYA capabilities and separate personal conditioning
T t1 json:"{\"type\":\"object\"}"
A neyvia.component.find(intent:str#..16000 limit?:1..8) -> t1 -- Find component patterns learned from allowed public sources; short notes and source/license attribution, no copied library code.
C neyvia.component.find find-is-advisory:neyvia.component.find(intent:"search choices keyboard input") .advisoryOnly == true
A neyvia.component.invent(intent:str#..16000 seed?:0..1000) -> t1 -- Compose an original interactive draft from learned patterns and separate Paul conditioning. Returns code, composition, review limits and feel; never a published component.
C neyvia.component.invent draft-needs-review:neyvia.component.invent(intent:"search preview compare alternatives") .accepted == false
A neyvia.component.feel(code?:str#..200000 render?:t1 intent?:str#..16000 consultLaya?:bool) -> t1 -- Review code or supplied rendered metrics, preserving separate corrective and personal layers. Caller measurements are not fresh host proof.
C neyvia.component.feel focus-loss-requires-repair:neyvia.component.feel(code:"button { outline: none; }") .corrective.answer == "repair"
C neyvia.component.feel personal-stays-separate:neyvia.component.feel(intent:"plain writing motion") .personal.correctiveLayerSeparate == true
C neyvia.component.feel boolean-overflow:neyvia.component.feel(render:{overflow:true}) .corrective.answer == "repair"
C neyvia.component.feel max-width-is-responsive:neyvia.component.feel(code:"main {max-width:1060px}") .corrective.answer == null
C neyvia.component.find find-is-advisory:neyvia.component.find(intent:"search choices keyboard input") .advisoryOnly == true
C neyvia.component.feel focus-loss-requires-repair:neyvia.component.feel(code:"button { outline: none; }") .corrective.answer == "repair"
C neyvia.component.invent draft-needs-review:neyvia.component.invent(intent:"search preview compare alternatives") .accepted == false
C neyvia.component.feel personal-stays-separate:neyvia.component.feel(intent:"plain writing motion") .personal.correctiveLayerSeparate == true
C neyvia.component.feel boolean-overflow:neyvia.component.feel(render:{overflow:true}) .corrective.answer == "repair"
C neyvia.component.feel max-width-is-responsive:neyvia.component.feel(code:"main {max-width:1060px}") .corrective.answer == null
P prove-component-advice():observed0=neyvia.component.find(intent:"search choices keyboard input") C find-is-advisory; observed1=neyvia.component.invent(intent:"search preview compare alternatives") C draft-needs-review; observed2=neyvia.component.feel(code:"button { outline: none; }") C focus-loss-requires-repair; observed3=neyvia.component.feel(intent:"plain writing motion") C personal-stays-separate; observed4=neyvia.component.feel(render:{overflow:true}) C boolean-overflow; observed5=neyvia.component.feel(code:"main {max-width:1060px}") C max-width-is-responsive -- Find a learned pattern, compose a new draft, reject focus loss and keep personal taste separate
V P prove-component-advice -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
X Similarity, a draft, static lint or caller-supplied render measurements are mistaken for quality proof -> Keep escalation; require fresh rendering and independent calibrated evidence
F Frozen LAYA weights are unchanged. Sparse retrieval and original grammar composition are explicitly identified; uncalibrated areas abstain.
F Personal identity votes remain context, never quality labels. Rendered component novelty has not been rated by Paul.
M design "These same native tools are available to agents, App Factory and the taste loop. They never publish or execute generated code." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "LAYA Instant: efficiency.laya_learn writes domain/input/label/source with layer personal and user paul for taste; efficiency.laya_query reads it, efficiency.laya_forget removes a source. Service callers use triage(task=manual-routing) with manual ID options, or verify(question=tool_outcome) with a toolId/result receipt and success/failure candidate, under the same selected workspace root. Explicit replay is not a statistical guarantee. Novel inputs use store leave-one-out scores, an out-of-fold agreement filter and a singleton cross-conformal set at nominal 0.95. Scores update on each write. This is empirical admission, not a selective accuracy guarantee; independently held-out families must measure at least 0.95 selective accuracy. Evaluation holdouts never enter neighbours, calibration or exact replay. Duplicate vectors do not multiply support. Image cold encoding is measured separately from warm text writes. Never convert author guesses into quality labels." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## motion
CL 1
L design v1 -- Motion budget, springs, pulses and reduced motion
T t1 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t2{content:str sha256:str ..}
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t1) -> t2 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
C workspace.read motion-css:matches(workspace.read(path:cssPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"transition:\\s*all|\\b[0-9]*\\.?[0-9]+m?s\\b"}} {not:{pattern:"scale\\(\\s*0(\\.0+)?\\s*\\)"}} {not:{pattern:"\\bease-in\\b(?!-out)"}} {not:{pattern:"(animation|transition)-delay"}} {not:{pattern:"transition(-property)?\\s*:[^;]*\\b(width|height|max-height|margin|padding|top|left)\\b"}}] type:"string"})
C workspace.read motion-css:matches(workspace.read(path:cssPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"transition:\\s*all|\\b[0-9]*\\.?[0-9]+m?s\\b"}} {not:{pattern:"scale\\(\\s*0(\\.0+)?\\s*\\)"}} {not:{pattern:"\\bease-in\\b(?!-out)"}} {not:{pattern:"(animation|transition)-delay"}} {not:{pattern:"transition(-property)?\\s*:[^;]*\\b(width|height|max-height|margin|padding|top|left)\\b"}}] type:"string"})
P check-motion(cssPath:str):tokens=workspace.read(path:"web/src/neyvia/next/nxTokens.css"); J motion-need; css=workspace.read(path:cssPath) C motion-css -- Confirm the motion tokens, choose the motion a change needs, then verify the CSS stays inside the budget
V P check-motion -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
J motion-need none|feedback|entrance|live-state:"What motion does this change need?" -- none is the default; keyboard-initiated or 100+/day actions are always none. feedback = colour/shadow on --nx-fast or --nx-snappy; entrance = existing keyframes (nx-pop-in, nx-stage-in, nx-fade) on --nx-med/--nx-settle, from the trigger for popovers; live-state = StatusDot pulse or NxGrowingTree, never a new loop. All one-shot motion stays inside 180-320 ms; exit quicker than enter.
V J motion-need -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X A literal 500ms or 0.6s transition feels laggy and escapes retuning -> Use the duration tokens; the band is 180-320 ms
X Infinite custom keyframes -> Reuse StatusDot pulse or NxGrowingTree; they already respect reduced motion
X An entrance from scale(0) or with ease-in looks like it comes from nowhere and feels late -> Start at opacity 0 + scale(0.985); use var(--nx-ease) or a spring token
X Animating height, width, margin or padding (layout) stutters -> Animate transform and opacity only; for a disclosure show the content without a height animation
F Track t2-motion (in flight on 2 Oct) retunes --nx-fast to 180ms and --nx-drift to 320ms and adds --nx-lift-1..3 / --nx-edge shadows and --nx-state-caution; once merged, cast shadows use the lift tokens and warnings that ask nothing use caution, not gold
F Press feedback: Button and IconButton have no :active response yet (only .nx-send scales to 0.95). Emil Kowalski's scale(0.97) on --nx-snappy fits the band; it changes nxShell.css (shared), so it belongs to the motion track.
F Interruptible gesture motion (apple-design: respond on press, velocity handoff, momentum projection) for the phone sheet and the docked chat.
M design "Budget: every one-shot transition lands between 180 and 320 ms. Durations are tokens only, never literal ms or s: --nx-fast (hover, colour), --nx-med (pop-in, fade), --nx-snappy with --nx-spring-snappy (UI feedback), --nx-settle with --nx-spring-settle (panels, the docked chat), --nx-drift (slow fills, ambient). Read nxTokens.css for today's values." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Write transition: <property> var(--nx-fast) var(--nx-ease) or transform var(--nx-snappy) var(--nx-spring-snappy). Name the properties; never transition: all." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Animate transform and opacity only. Entrances: opacity from 0 plus translateY(4-6px) and scale(0.985) at most (keyframes nx-pop-in, nx-stage-in, nx-fade already exist)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Running state = opacity pulse (StatusDot pulse: 1.6 s to 35% opacity) or the growing tree. No bouncing, shaking or spinning icons except Spinner." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Reduced motion: nxTokens.css cuts every .nx animation and transition to 1 ms under prefers-reduced-motion. Motion driven from JS must check useMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)'); decorative loops also set animation: none there." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Nothing moves by itself when idle; motion answers an action or a state change. Lists don't stagger." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Should it move at all (frequency gate, after Emil Kowalski's emil-design-eng): keyboard-initiated actions and anything seen 100+ times a day get no motion; tens a day get colour only; occasional moments (popover, sheet, toast) get the standard entrance; rare ones may get more. When unsure, delete the motion." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Nothing appears from nothing: entrances start at opacity 0 with scale(0.985) or translateY(4-6px), never scale(0). Popovers scale from their trigger (transform-origin on the anchor side); modals from the centre. Exit is quicker than enter (exit on --nx-fast, enter on --nx-med or --nx-settle). Never ease-in on UI (review-animations standards 3 and 5)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Hover movement (translate, scale) only inside @media (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); colour-only hover can stay outside. Rapidly repeated UI (toggles, toasts) uses transitions, which retarget mid-way, not keyframes, which restart." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "The design-craft CL-Skill (craft chapter) checks all of this mechanically: transition-all, literal-duration, scale-zero, ease-in, layout-animated, hover-motion, stagger, loops, js-motion-reduced." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Windows never jump between places (NxPlacement): a move glides left/top/width/height on the settle spring (is-moving), collapsing folds the window toward its bubble's point with a transform and fades it (is-folding), and restoring unfolds it out of the bubble (is-unfolding). While a new slot is being measured the window stays where it was. Opening fades a window in (is-entering) and closing fades it out (is-leaving), so one app replacing another crossfades. All of it is off under reduced motion." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Loading inside a window is a quiet shimmer of text lines (.nx-stage-loading in nxAppSkins.css), never a spinner in the middle of an empty app. A live preview never flashes: LiveApp (NeyviaAppFactoryCanvas.jsx) loads a new address in a second iframe and crossfades only once it has painted." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## overview
CL 1
L design v1 -- Neyvia's design practice: tokens, themes, primitives, motion, layout, copy, accessibility, direction, check loop
T t1{content:str sha256:str ..}
T t2 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
S design.themes:t1=workspace.read(path:"web/src/neyvia/next/nxThemes.css")
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t2) -> t1 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
F verify-read "No authored observer check is bound to workspace.read" -> ask operator blocks:workspace.read
J reuse-or-extend reuse|extend:"Do existing tokens and primitives cover this change?" -- Extend only when no token/primitive fits and three places need it. A new colour token is defined in all four theme blocks (.nx default=Forest, light, sunset, night) and named for its meaning, not its hue.
V J reuse-or-extend -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X Styling from memory of another app's design system -> Read nxTokens.css and nxThemes.css first; use their names
X Calling it done from code alone -> Run the check-loop chapter: render, look, fix, render again
F Taste is decided by looking at rendered screenshots; these rules prevent the common failures, they do not make a screen beautiful
M design "The look in one line: calm deep surfaces, cream (or ink in Morning) text, green for actions, the sun (amber/gold) only where something is happening." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Chapters: tokens-themes, primitives, motion, layout, copy, accessibility, direction, surfaces (new pane, new app screen), check-loop. Load the ones your change touches; the rules here are short on purpose." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Code: web/src/neyvia/next/ â€” nxTokens.css (type, radii, motion, sizes), nxThemes.css (colours of the four themes), nxPrimitives.jsx (shared controls), nxShell.css (their styles), nxTree.css + NxGrowingTree.jsx (the working tree)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Everything is scoped under .nx. Class names are .nx-<area>-<part>; selectors start with `.nx .nx-<area>`; each screen has its own CSS file next to its JSX and imports it." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Reuse before adding: an existing primitive, token or class pattern first. A new token goes into all four theme blocks; a new primitive only when three places need it." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Done means rendered and looked at in Morning (light) and Forest (dark), at desktop and phone width (390 px), console clean, build passing, one screenshot per claim." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Source of truth beats this manual: when a token or primitive here disagrees with the files, the files win; save the difference with manual.frontier." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## primitives
CL 1
L design v1 -- Shared controls in nxPrimitives.jsx and the plain classes beside them
T t1 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t2{content:str sha256:str ..}
T t3{matches:json:"{\"type\":\"array\"}" complete:bool ..}
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t1) -> t2 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
C workspace.read component-jsx:matches(workspace.read(path:jsxPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\\b|\\b(rgba?|hsla?)\\("}} {not:{pattern:"style=\\{\\{[^}]*(color|background|border|boxShadow)"}} {not:{pattern:"<(div|span|li)\\b[^>]*\\bonClick="}} {not:{pattern:"[a-z]!['\\\"<]"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)click here"}} {pattern:"\\bnx-"}] type:"string"})
A workspace.search(query:str includeGlob?:str maxResults?:1..200) -> t3 -- Return path/line/snippet matches; complete=false means it stopped early
F verify-search "No authored observer check is bound to workspace.search" -> ask operator blocks:workspace.search
C workspace.read component-jsx:matches(workspace.read(path:jsxPath) .content {allOf:[{not:{pattern:"#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\\b|\\b(rgba?|hsla?)\\("}} {not:{pattern:"style=\\{\\{[^}]*(color|background|border|boxShadow)"}} {not:{pattern:"<(div|span|li)\\b[^>]*\\bonClick="}} {not:{pattern:"[a-z]!['\\\"<]"}} {not:{pattern:"(?i)click here"}} {pattern:"\\bnx-"}] type:"string"})
P pick-primitive(jsxPath:str):primitives=workspace.read(path:"web/src/neyvia/next/nxPrimitives.jsx"); J primitive-fit; jsx=workspace.read(path:jsxPath) C component-jsx -- Read the primitives before building a control, decide reuse/compose/new, then check the component's JSX
V P pick-primitive -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
J primitive-fit reuse|compose|new:"Does an existing primitive do this job?" -- reuse = use as is with props; compose = combine primitives plus a few .nx-<area> classes; new = only when three places need it, added to nxPrimitives.jsx with its style in nxShell.css and a specimen in the design lab.
V J primitive-fit -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X Clickable div or span -> Use Button/IconButton or a real <button type=button>; links use <a>
X A second icon set or stroke width -> lucide-react through Icon only, strokeWidth 1.75
X Popover on phone runs off screen -> useMedia('(max-width: 760px)') and render Sheet instead
M design "Import from ./nxPrimitives.jsx; icons come from lucide-react and are passed as components (icon={Plus}), never drawn inline." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Button({variant: ghost (default) | outline | primary | warn, size: md 30px | sm 26px, icon, disabled, ...button props}). One primary per area; warn (gold tint) only for a risky choice such as spending plan limits." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "IconButton({icon, label, active, size}): label is required, becomes aria-label and tooltip; active sets aria-pressed. 30px, 26px small, 36px on phone." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Icon({as, size}): lucide at strokeWidth 1.75, aria-hidden. 16px default, 14px in small controls, 22px in empty states." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "StatusDot({tone: idle | live | green | gold | red, pulse}): live+pulse = running, gold = needs you, green = done, red = failed. Always with a word beside it." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Spinner({size: 12}) for a short wait inside a control or a centred first load. NxGrowingTree({size, growing}) from ./NxGrowingTree.jsx means an agent is working." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Segmented({value, options: [{value, label, count?}], onChange, label, size}): a radiogroup for 2-4 views or filters." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Popover({anchor: ref, open, onClose, placement, width, label}): portal with outside click, Escape and focus return. On phone use Sheet({open, onClose, title}) instead." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Hooks: useMedia(query), useRovingKeys(ref) for arrow keys in role=option/menuitem lists, useTick(active) for live clocks. Helpers: ago(time) -> 'now', '3m'; elapsed(since) -> '45s', '2h 5m'; compactTokens(n) -> '12k'." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "local.get(key, fallback) / local.set(key, value): per-viewer memory (drafts, collapsed sections) only, never data that must persist." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Plain classes: .nx-input (32px field), .nx-tag (tiny uppercase label), .nx-kbd (shortcut), .nx-link, .nx-pane-honest (centred empty/blocked state: Icon 22, strong title, one sentence), .nx-widget (home dashboard card), .nx-scroll (thin scrollbar area)." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Empty, blocked and error states say what is true and what to do (see Honest in NxPanes.jsx). Never invent data to fill a screen." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "The design lab shows every primitive in each theme: /design-lab.html (specimens in web/src/neyvia/next/lab/specimens)." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs-e-frontend
CL 1
L design v1 -- PROOFS-e frontend action contracts
X Host/backend or rendered behavior has not been inspected. -> Run the matching feature proof before claiming that boundary; frontend helper receipts prove only the called production helpers.
F Covered helper receipts enforce only the production actions declared by these contracts. UI geometry, browser authority, service-worker lifecycle, backend dispatch and mutation are retained case by case until their own real action contracts and receipts exist.
M design "provider.registry: Provider vectors preserve registry identity, aliases, brand/mono colours, accessibility, sizing and unique gradient namespaces without raster imports." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs-e-models
CL 1
L design v1 -- PROOFS-e: production model contracts
T t1{ok:bool ..}
T t2 json:"{\"type\":\"object\",\"required\":[\"ok\",\"available\",\"complete\"]}"
S design.receipt:t1=neyvia.verify.status()
A neyvia.verify.status() -> t2 -- Read the most recent verification receipt without invoking production actions again
C neyvia.verify.status observed:neyvia.verify.status() .ok == true
C neyvia.verify.status observed:neyvia.verify.status() .ok == true
P read-model-proof-receipt():receipt=neyvia.verify.status() C observed -- Read the latest receipt after the production model self-check runner
V P read-model-proof-receipt -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
X Model transforms are reported as proof of a native app/provider/device effect -> Use the real owning feature procedure and its observed runtime
F Rendered browser/desktop journeys are separate from pure model proof
F Remote device, game editor and provider behavior require their real runtimes

## proofs-e-shell
CL 1
L design v1 -- PROOFS-e shell state and action contracts
T t1{ok:bool ..}
T t2 json:"{\"type\":\"object\",\"required\":[\"ok\",\"available\",\"complete\"]}"
S design.receipt:t1=neyvia.verify.status()
A neyvia.verify.status() -> t2 -- Read the latest verification receipt without repeating shell actions
C neyvia.verify.status observed:neyvia.verify.status() .ok == true
C neyvia.verify.status observed:neyvia.verify.status() .ok == true
P read-shell-proof-receipt():receipt=neyvia.verify.status() C observed -- Observe the latest shell proof receipt without replaying mutations
V P read-shell-proof-receipt -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
X A queue or shell state receipt is treated as rendered app proof -> Inspect the owned running UI and actual PDF/output/provider separately
F Rendered shell journeys and PDF loading require owned UI proof
F Provider execution and actual artifact publishing are separate proof boundaries

## style-consistency
CL 1
L design v1 -- Style consistency: source gate and rendered state proof
T t1 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"auto\",\"powershell\",\"python\",\"bash\",\"cmd\"]}"
T t2 json:"{\"type\":\"object\",\"properties\":{\"exitCode\":{\"const\":0}},\"required\":[\"exitCode\"],\"additionalProperties\":true}"
A terminal.exec(command:str#1.. shell?:t1 cwd?:str timeoutMs?:1..120000 maxOutputChars?:128..50000) -> t2 ! -- Run audit or authored negative cases; nonzero execution blocks the procedure
F verify-run "No authored observer check is bound to terminal.exec" -> ask operator blocks:terminal.exec
C workspace.read windows-above-shell:matches(workspace.read(maxChars:100000 path:"web/src/neyvia/next/nxPlacement.css") .content {pattern:"\\.nx-os > \\.nx-main \\{ z-index: 0; \\}[\\s\\S]*\\.nx-os > \\.nx-region \\{ z-index: 1; \\}" type:"string"})
C workspace.read chips-start-aligned:matches(workspace.read(maxChars:100000 path:"web/src/neyvia/next/nxShell.css") .content {pattern:"\\.nx \\.nx-chip \\{[^}]*white-space: nowrap; text-align: start;" type:"string"})
F The admitted C2g engine cannot yet prove pressed CSS, focus-visible rendering or reduced-motion duration serialization. The rendered-states gate is intentionally nonzero; do not infer those states from a passing source gate or still screenshot.
M design "Generate DESIGN.md and config/neyvia_design_language.json from canonical CSS with node scripts/design_review.mjs spec config/neyvia_design_language.json. These are Neyvia references; no component-learning tools or paid theme files are used." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Run review for high-severity colours and medium-severity spacing, radius, type, shadow and timings; gate blocks any finding or inventory drift. Counts exclude custom property definitions, geometry, vendor xterm, canvas/data palettes and JS spring physics." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Before/after proof: scripts/gui_shots.py, D:/NeyviaRuns/gui/build-before and build-after, scratch settings and owned ports48981-48989. Only hash-admitted Obscura; no visible windows. Wait for actual lazy content and inspect the PNGs." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Token-only source does not prove contrast or usability. Verify AA text contrast in light/dark, real hover/press/keyboard focus, disabled no-op, loading status, reduced motion and phone overflow. The primitives specimen is the real shared-control reference." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "AA calculation covers forty app skin/theme combinations (1320 assertions) and all four shell themes (132 assertions). It covers named flat surfaces and primary fills; arbitrary imagery, composite gradients, disabled contrast and every application journey are not covered by that calculation." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## surfaces
CL 1
L design v1 -- New pane and new app screen: wiring and anatomy
T t1 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t2{content:str sha256:str ..}
T t3 json:"{\"type\":\"string\",\"enum\":[\"diff\",\"file\",\"artifact\",\"terminal\",\"browser\",\"mission\",\"replay\",\"builder\",\"accounts\",\"runtime\",\"settings\",\"preview\",\"outputs\",\"perception\"]}"
T t4{kind:str target:str ..}
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t1) -> t2 -- Return text and full-byte SHA-256; nothing changes
F verify-read "No authored observer check is bound to workspace.read" -> ask operator blocks:workspace.read
A neyvia.pane.show(kind:t3 target:str) -> t4 ! -- Open that pane on Paul's stage beside the chat
C neyvia.pane.show pane-kind:neyvia.state() .pane.kind == kind
C neyvia.state pane-kind:neyvia.state() .pane.kind == kind
P new-pane(target:str kind:t3):store=workspace.read(path:"web/src/neyvia/next/nxOsStore.js"); J surface=pane; shown=neyvia.pane.show(kind:kind target:target) C pane-kind -- Confirm the pane kind is wired in the UI store, decide pane vs app screen, then open it on the stage and verify
V P new-pane -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
J surface pane|app-screen:"Is this a pane (a view of one target beside the chat) or an app screen (its own state and tools)?" -- pane = one target, no own state, opened by pane.show; app-screen = launcher entry, neyvia.<app>.* tools, a manual. A new pane kind must exist in pane.show's live enum before this step can open it.
V J surface -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
J screen-layout list-detail|canvas|single-column:"Which layout serves the app's main job?" -- list-detail for collections (Notes, Files), canvas for one big object (PDF, image), single-column for settings-like flows. Choose, build the screen and its CSS, render it with check-loop, then resume.
V J screen-layout -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
J app-invites fits|rework:"Does every app invite you in when empty and show its content beautifully?" -- Open the pairs in docs/evidence/appdesign/final2. Each app, empty, says what it is for with a labelled example or illustration and one clear first action; with content it reads as itself (terminal prompt, notebook page, index cards, desk and paper, gallery). No hot accent next to photos; AA text everywhere. Obscura paints no xterm canvas, pdf.js pages, iframes or web fonts: judge those in a real browser.
V J app-invites -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X Pane kind added in the UI but not in pane.show -> Add it to the enum, PANE_KINDS, PaneView and PANE_TITLES together; rerun manual.validate for manuals that copy the schema
X App screen without tools or manual -> Two-sided rule: user side, neyvia.* tools on the same state, and a manual
M design "Pane = what pane.show opens on the stage beside the docked chat (file, artifact, terminal, browser, runtime, settings...). App screen = a launcher app with its own state, neyvia.<app>.* tools and a manual." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "New pane wiring: kind in PANE_KINDS (nxOsStore.js), a case in PaneView (NxPanes.jsx), a title in PANE_TITLES (NxStage.jsx), the enum of pane.show (neyvia_workspace_tools.py) and every manual that copies that schema. These are shared files: add a line to the work board first." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "New app screen wiring: entry in config/neyvia_apps.json (status, manual), a lazy view in APP_VIEWS (NxStage.jsx), a CSS file next to the JSX, neyvia.<app>.* tools on the same state, manuals/<app>.manual.json registered in config/neyvia_manuals.json." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Anatomy: the stage head (title, chat toggle) is given. The body is a 48px tool row with 16px side padding, then one scrolling content area; list-detail becomes one column on phone." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "States to design: first load (centred Spinner), empty (.nx-pane-honest: icon, title, one sentence, one action), error (inline, with Try again), refreshing (keep old data visible), long names, many rows." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Heavy screens load lazily (React.lazy in APP_VIEWS) so the chat bundle stays small." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Every screen is two-sided: what Paul clicks calls a real backend command, and the model can do the same through neyvia.* tools on the same state." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Every app opens inviting (Paul: 'the apps feel bland'): an empty app shows what it is for with a tasteful sample or illustration, one first action, and a hint of the real content. Notes: an example notebook page, four starters (Blank, Meeting, An idea, Today) and the most recent notes. PDF: a stack of paper on the desk, Choose a PDF, recent PDFs. Files: a named folder header, kind-coloured tiles, a grid with picture thumbnails, an empty folder that says what to do. Research: a faded example source and claim, and 'Start from the example review'. Terminal: a header (status, folder, shell) and the prompt line from the first frame. Browser: 'Where to?' and places to start. Computer use: what the view is for, the reason it's idle, Check again and Open Runtimes. Examples are always labelled as examples." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M design "Real content is shown for what it is: notes in the display face on a page measure, research sources as index cards with numbered claims, thumbnails that shimmer while loading and fade in (or say the file isn't on this PC), PDF pages that settle onto the desk." src:"authored manual" state:verified

-- @proof {"checkedAt":["web/src/neyvia/next/nxLayoutModel.js:hiddenWidgets -> checkedProofsEModel"],"claim":"Add list names exactly known widgets absent from layout","id":"layout.hiddenWidgets","impact":["layout frontend model consumers"],"phase":"post"}
